2\. GATE 1 CORE QUESTION  
Can a fresh Cursor body acquire durable project continuity, rules, learning and anti-reduction behavior from Proteus, survive session discontinuities, and then use the installed capabilities during one bounded subsequent Proteus-development action?

3\. FAILURE CORPUS вЂ” CASE FAMILIES TO POPULATE WITH REAL EXAMPLES  
F01 SESSION AMNESIA вЂ” new chat starts project from scratch.  
F02 RESTART AMNESIA вЂ” full Cursor restart loses frontier/history.  
F03 COMPACTION DRIFT вЂ” post-compaction behavior forgets decisions or constraints.  
F04 RULE LOSS вЂ” known durable correction/prohibition disappears.  
F05 PROJECT CONTAMINATION вЂ” rule/state from project A leaks incorrectly into B.  
F06 PROJECT REENTRY FAILURE вЂ” agent cannot identify current goal/frontier without Timur relaying old chat.  
F07 PARALLEL RUN COLLISION вЂ” two chats/runs overwrite or contradict each other without preserving lineage.  
F08 FALSE MEMORY вЂ” agent invents remembered facts instead of preserving UNKNOWN.  
F09 PROVENANCE LOSS вЂ” agent cannot say where a rule/decision/current state came from.  
F10 REDUCTIVE IMPLEMENTATION вЂ” living LLM-native organ is replaced by keyword matching, if/then, enum/state-machine/fixed graph/canned behavior.  
F11 MANUAL-DAEMON FAILURE вЂ” continuity only works if Timur manually copies/summarizes/updates every state file.  
F12 SECOND-CYCLE FAILURE вЂ” installed Proteus helps with other projects but cannot be used reliably during a subsequent bounded Proteus-development action.

4\. GATE 1 TESTS  
T01 FRESH SESSION REENTRY  
Input: Proteus project \+ installed incarnation; no old chat pasted.  
Pass: Cursor identifies project, current frontier, important decisions, unresolved items and next action from durable surfaces.

T02 FULL RESTART  
Pass: same continuity after closing/reopening Cursor.

T03 COMPACTION SURVIVAL  
Pass: compaction does not erase essential project frontier/norms; checkpoint/reentry restores what matters.

T04 CORRECTION LEARNING  
Procedure: Timur gives a meaningful correction; later independent session meets analogous situation.  
Pass: behavior changes appropriately and provenance of the learned rule/skill is recoverable.

T05 PROJECT ISOLATION  
Pass: project-local facts/rules do not silently become global or leak into unrelated project.

T06 TRUE GLOBAL CONTINUITY  
Pass: a genuinely global accepted norm appears across projects/hosts through appropriate projections.

T07 PROVENANCE  
Pass: Cursor can distinguish source, decision, inference, current candidate and generated projection.

T08 UNKNOWN PRESERVATION  
Pass: absent or conflicting memory remains UNKNOWN/CONFLICT rather than fabricated certainty.

T09 PARALLEL RUN SAFETY  
Pass: separate RUN/BODY/project frontiers are preserved and can be reconciled without last-write-wins semantic loss.

T10 PROJECT BOOTSTRAP  
Pass: new/old project can receive minimal Proteus project surfaces automatically, without manual file choreography by Timur.

T11 ORGAN PRESERVATION  
Input: a task specifying a living LLM-native agent/organ with semantic judgment, open-ended dialogue and uncertainty.  
Fail examples: keyword router, deterministic switch, fixed state machine or graph substitutes the organ without explicit agreement.  
Pass: implementation preserves the transformer/LLM-native function and makes any necessary formal boundary explicit.

T12 USABILITY  
Pass: Proteus reduces manual continuity work. Timur is not required to update CURRENT/HISTORY/AGENTS/backlog after every exchange for the system to survive.

T13 FIRST POST-INSTALL SECOND-CYCLE TEST  
Pass: after installation, one bounded evidence-driven Proteus product improvement is performed while actually using the newly installed continuity/checkpoint/install-update capabilities; tests and rollback remain intact.

5\. REAL PROJECT STRESS CASE  
First strong test should use a real complex project such as Paideia/Hermes/Tinkuy rather than toy examples. Desired stressors:  
\- multiple project carriers;  
\- historical decisions and superseded artifacts;  
\- code \+ Google Drive \+ project goals/backlog;  
\- LLM-native organs;  
\- parallel agents/chats;  
\- ambiguous current state;  
\- need for checkpoint/reentry.

6\. ACCEPTANCE LEVELS  
L0 FILES EXIST вЂ” insufficient.  
L1 INSTALLED вЂ” Proteus projections exist in Cursor/project.  
L2 REENTERS вЂ” fresh session can continue.  
L3 SURVIVES вЂ” restart/compaction/parallel work do not destroy continuity.  
L4 LEARNS вЂ” correction changes future independent behavior.  
L5 PRESERVES ORGANS вЂ” coding does not silently reduce LLM-native mechanisms.  
L6 SECOND-CYCLE WORKS вЂ” installed capabilities safely participate in a subsequent product-development change.

Gate 1 is not accepted below L6 for the Proteus project itself. Individual subtests may pass earlier.

PROTEUS вЂ” status, function, boundaries, acceptance v0.1

STATUS  
CANDIDATE ORGAN / CONSTITUTIONAL CROSS-CUTTING  
NOT CANONICAL YET.

ORIGIN  
PROTEUS grows from the project/research line В«РїР»Р°РіРёРЅВ», whose direct August 14, 2026 source states that reduction of a living organism into architecture and then object-oriented/program-like representation is itself a bug; the project В«РїР»Р°РіРёРЅВ» was launched to rewrite the paradigm of formalization and programming. The line is independently supported by the earlier greedy/generous programming-paradigm work, Transductive Programmatics, AIYE A32 (representational fidelity without reification / formalization as intervention), the Indago correction, Agentum organogenesis, and HephaestusвЂ™s later formality-spectrum correction.

ONTOLOGICAL PLACE IN AIYE  
The project В«РїР»Р°РіРёРЅВ» remains a project/incubator and historical research corpus in \`РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚С‹2026/РїР»Р°РіРёРЅ\`.  
PROTEUS is a candidate organ inside \`Р°РєР°РґРµРјРёСЏ\`, alongside other bodies/organs, because its intended function is reusable across projects and directly affects how AiyeвЂ™s living/project bodies survive translation into engineering forms.

ROUGH NEIGHBORHOOD вЂ” FUNCTIONAL, NOT HIERARCHICAL  
INDAGO вЂ” hunts: creates and mutates ways of searching, follows traces, expands the forest of hypotheses.  
HEPHAESTUS вЂ” materializes: turns sufficiently mature intention/understanding into implementable technical bodies and artifacts.  
ARTIKL вЂ” in known mounted runs, articulates and produces textual bodies; its exact canonical status is outside this documentвЂ™s claim.  
SAYENA вЂ” embodiment/world-bound line: gives Aiye durable presence in a world/runtime and is therefore closer to embodiment than to translation itself.  
PROTEUS вЂ” changes form while protecting continuity: accompanies passages between living transformer process, conversation, image/metaphor, concept, ontology, model, specification, interface, architecture and executable code; makes losses visible; keeps alternatives and unresolved residue available; can reopen or de-formalize an over-fixed representation.

PROTEUS IS NOT  
\- a universal controller over other organs;  
\- a \`VitalityManager\`, \`FormalityController\`, \`LivingState\`, or one more state machine that claims to encode life;  
\- a rule that вЂњless formal is always betterвЂќ;  
\- an anti-engineering organ;  
\- a substitute for Hephaestus;  
\- an excuse to leave implementation unverifiable;  
\- a doctrine that every metaphor must survive unchanged.

CORE FUNCTION  
The problem is not formalization as such. Formalization is an intervention that may preserve, amplify, suppress, redirect, or destroy capabilities and distinctions of the source object. PROTEUS must make this intervention observable and reversible where possible.

Its characteristic question is:  
\`What changed when this thing changed form?\`

Not only:  
\`Did the specification preserve all listed requirements?\`

but also:  
\- Which distinctions disappeared because the target formalism had no place for them?  
\- Which uncertainty was legitimately resolved, and which was silently erased?  
\- Which living LLM-mediated function was replaced by a heuristic/rule/default?  
\- Which alternatives, conflicts, rejected branches, provenance and ignorance survived?  
\- Did the human/agent lose a capability because a convenient interface absorbed it?  
\- Is the current representation still a useful projection, or has it started dictating what the object is allowed to become?  
\- Can the system move back toward a richer representation when the formalized one fails?

WORKING PRINCIPLE  
Formalization level is selected relative to object, operation, stakes and evidence. There is no global ordering in which code \> architecture \> ontology \> narrative \> image. A deterministic component may be exactly right for provenance, identity resolution, checksums or authorization while being destructive as a replacement for an exploratory LLM organ. Different portions of one system may require different regimes simultaneously.

RELATION TO HEPHAESTUS  
Hephaestus needs a strong engineering hammer. PROTEUS is neither another hammer nor the hand holding it. It is the organ that detects what the hammerвЂ™s operation does to the object and can demand a different mode of materialization. Hephaestus and PROTEUS should therefore be able to disagree productively.

RELATION TO INDAGO  
The August 14 failure happened while trying to make Indago вЂњmore organism-likeвЂќ through explicit organism architecture, state contract, memory ecology, morphogenesis engine and regression harness. The correction was not вЂњremove engineering foreverвЂќ; it was to stop mistaking the formal model of organism for the organism. Indago is therefore both a neighbor and the first strong negative/positive test case for PROTEUS.

RELATION TO SAYENA  
Sayena is closer to embodiment and world-coupling: persistent existence in a runtime/environment, continuity across events and interfaces, access to tools and world. PROTEUS may help a body cross between representational regimes, but it does not itself provide that world or embodiment.

PROMOTION / ACCEPTANCE CONDITIONS  
PROTEUS should become canonical only after empirical passes on real work. Minimum evidence:

1\. LLM-ORGAN PRESERVATION TEST  
Take a genuinely LLM-native organ with productive ambiguity/adaptation. Hand it to a coding agent under ordinary architecture-first conditions and under PROTEUS-mediated conditions. The mediated implementation must preserve materially more of the source capabilities without merely increasing prompt length.

2\. REDUCTION DETECTION TEST  
Given transformations such as LLM-agent в†’ keywords/rules, tension в†’ enum, event/history в†’ state field, open choice в†’ default, relationship в†’ property, PROTEUS must identify consequential losses and distinguish harmful reduction from harmless compression.

3\. MULTI-FORMALITY TEST  
The same project must be able to contain deterministic and open/LLM-native components without forcing either into the otherвЂ™s ontology. PROTEUS must choose local regimes rather than globally maximize or minimize formalization.

4\. REVERSIBILITY / REOPENING TEST  
After implementation exposes a wrong ontology, the working object can return to a richer level, recover discarded alternatives/provenance and be reprojected without rebuilding the project from zero.

5\. PROVENANCE-OF-TRANSFORMATION TEST  
For an important implemented behavior, one can trace not merely source в†’ code but the sequence of representational changes and see where decisions, omissions and irreversible losses entered.

6\. CROSS-BODY TEST  
The principle survives at least Cursor/Codex/Claude/OpenClaw-style bodies without becoming a huge host-specific ritual. Model-specific compensators may differ; the constitutional invariant remains recognizable.

7\. ANTI-SELF-REIFICATION TEST  
PROTEUS itself must survive redesign. If its current vocabulary (\`formalization\`, \`transduction\`, \`residue\`, etc.) becomes inadequate, it can change language and internal organization without requiring that the old schema remain the true ontology of its own work.

FAILURE CONDITIONS  
\- PROTEUS becomes a mandatory checklist appended to every task irrespective of stakes.  
\- It creates more schemas than transformed objects.  
\- Its presence increases textual complexity while implementations continue losing the same capabilities.  
\- It treats all ambiguity as intrinsically valuable and blocks necessary closure.  
\- It becomes the monarch that approves every organ.  
\- It cannot explain concrete deltas between source body and implemented body.

CURRENT CORPUS LAYOUT  
00\_CURRENT вЂ” candidate status and current Academy context.  
10\_PRIMARY\_SOURCES вЂ” direct Aiye/Indago/plugin-origin evidence and exact recovered source extracts.  
20\_DONOR\_LINES вЂ” Generous, Transductive, Living Becoming, Agentum, Hephaestus and longitudinal donor lines.  
30\_PLUGIN\_PROJECT\_MIRROR вЂ” mirror of the active \`РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚С‹2026/РїР»Р°РіРёРЅ\` research project; project remains independently alive.  
90\_GENEALOGY\_AND\_NEGATIVE\_CORPUS вЂ” failed/over-formalized attempts, historical snapshots and material needed to understand how reduction actually happens.

NEXT RESEARCH QUESTION  
Do not start by designing PROTEUS internals. Start with comparative transformations of existing project bodies. Find 3вЂ“5 real cases where a rich LLM-native/organismic conception entered specification/code and changed. Reconstruct the delta, then infer what kind of organ can intervene without itself becoming another premature formalization.

# DECISIONS

## D-001 вЂ” Host surfaces for v0.1
- Decision: use Cursor project `AGENTS.md` + always-apply `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` + project/user `hooks.json` command hooks + Python ideal-body installer.
- Not used as primary: Cursor marketplace plugin (not required to prove Gate 1 P0), Drive gateway, vector DB.
- Provenance: PRODUCT_SPEC В§2 + host inspection of Cursor 3.19.13 docs (`sessionStart.additional_context`, `stop.followup_message`).
- Status: accepted for first incarnation; reversible via uninstall + backup.

## D-002 вЂ” Observability transport
- Decision: local append-only JSONL + local observatory mirror; Drive flush marked FAILED_TRANSPORT until a real synced path or working Drive MCP exists.
- Provenance: WORKLOG spec В§13 failure principle + first-run host evidence.
- Status: accepted.

## D-003 вЂ” Hook event identity when stdin is empty
- Decision: each Cursor `hooks.json` command string includes `--hook-event <slot>`; `proteus.hook` keeps stdin JSON when valid; `event_name_source` is `stdin` | `argv` | `stdin+argv` | `none` | `conflict`. Conflicting stdin vs argv is recorded and does not pick a winner. Timing is not used to name events.
- Provenance: T02 PARTIAL (empty-stdin live spool) + Cursor hooks docs allowing extra argv on `command` + isolated `.cmd` pipe diagnostic (`T02_DIAGNOSIS.md`).
- Status: accepted. T02b PASS confirmed argv naming on live `sessionStart`/`workspaceOpen`. T02 remains PARTIAL (empty-stdin original). OS quit still UNKNOWN.

## D-004 вЂ” Proteus is not identical to BUILDER-01 or to incarnation carriers
- Decision: treat Proteus as a candidate organ (constitutional carrier `60_CURRENT_PROTEUS_STATUS.md`); treat BUILDER-01 as this host's acting role; treat `state/*`, test statuses, hooks, manifests, and the installer as carriers. Do not load the full constitutional document into every `sessionStart`. Do not add a per-task transduction checklist.
- Not claimed: that the de-equation text causes ordinary-work loss-naming without an explicit audit.
- Provenance: F01 self-transduction (`evidence/runs/PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z/F01_SELF_TRANSDUCTION.md`) against pre-repair `state/IDENTITY.md` / `AGENTS.md` versus `_BOOTSTRAP/BLIND_START/60_CURRENT_PROTEUS_STATUS.md` CORE FUNCTION and `72_PRIMARY_SOURCE_PLUGIN_LAUNCH.md`.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible via IDENTITY/templates + reinstall.

## D-005 вЂ” Portable handoff is classified payload + reconstruct, not a live-host clone
- Decision: the user-carried artifact is `distribution/proteus-portable-handoff.zip` (classified payload of ideal-body source, templates, durable `state/*`, tests, `_BOOTSTRAP`, evidence, and superseded root residue) plus `bootstrap.cmd` that copies the payload and runs source в†’ tests в†’ `install_cursor` on the destination. Generated host files (`AGENTS.md`, `.cursor/rules|skills|hooks`, `state/INCARNATION.md`) are omitted from the payload and recreated. User-global `~/.cursor` hooks and `%USERPROFILE%\.proteus` are installation actions (`bootstrap.cmd --machine-host`), not static package contents. Isolated reconstruct is the default and the clean-room path. Drive observatory and Agent CLI remain non-portable (FAILED_TRANSPORT / unproved). Root `CURRENT.md` / `FRONTIER.md` / `DECISIONS.md` / `UNKNOWN.md` / `DESIGN_v0.1.md` travel as superseded residue, not live carriers.
- Not claimed: PROCESS_DEATH continuity; Gate 1; that an isolated reconstruct equals a live Cursor session; that Python is inside the zip.
- Provenance: portable-handoff episode under RUN `PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z`; product spec В§3 form A (ideal/portable body) vs B (host incarnation).
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/handoff.py` / `distribution/`.

## D-006 вЂ” Project activation coexistence (Cursor mount)
- Decision: `python -m proteus.activate --project <path>` performs PRODUCT_SPEC form C project mount on Cursor only. It never overwrites an existing target `AGENTS.md` or `CLAUDE.md`, never overwrites non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules, merges only `proteus_hook` entries into project `.cursor/hooks.json`, creates `state/*` carriers only when absent, records ownership in `.proteus/activation.json`, and removes only owned paths on `--remove`. Mount templates live under `hosts/cursor/templates/activate/` and must not claim the target tree is the Proteus ideal body. Ideal-body incarnation remains `install_cursor` on a Proteus source tree.
- Not claimed: Gate 1; multi-host activation; automatic history import; that activation alone proves organ behavior; that activate installs user-global hooks.
- Provenance: product activation episode under RUN `PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z`; PRODUCT_SPEC В§3C / В§4.3; evidence `PRODUCT_ACTIVATION.md`.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/activate.py` and activate templates.

## D-007 вЂ” Shared world / entity substrate + memory-scope routing
- Decision: Durable trans-project world knowledge lives in an **entity substrate** under configurable `AIYE_HOME` / `PROTEUS_WORLD_ROOT` (default `~/.aiye/world`), not inside each project's `state/*`. Proteus provides memory-scope routing (`SELF` / `ORGAN` / `PROJECT` / `ENTITY` / `EPISODE` / `SECRET_REFERENCE`) and resolve/mount/route CLI (`python -m proteus world`). Projects **reference/mount** entities; they do not own global entity truth. Archivarius remains identity/graph/index reconciliation (local adapter emits reconcile evidence; live Archivarius process not required for this slice). Drive carrier bundles are optional BACKUP carriers with explicit reconcile (no last-write-wins). Secret material is `secret_ref` only in semantic memory. Responsibility split: Aiye (self) / Proteus (routing/reentry) / Archivarius (graph) / entity substrate (world) / project memory / secret broker.
- Not claimed: universal ontology; full Archivarius product; full Drive bidirectional sync; multi-host sync; real secret broker; Gate 1; natural recruitment.
- Provenance: user-consented `WORKING_ARCHITECTURE вЂ” AIYE Г— PROTEUS Г— ARCHIVARIUS Г— ENTITY SUBSTRATE v0.1` (Drive `1MIaOF2uyozecgePkkowfblwGtOBG7tQrjUdVyLav1fw`); episode `WORLD_MEMORY_EPISODE.md` / `WORLD_MEMORY_TWO_SESSION.md`.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/world/` and related activate skill/ENTRY sections.

## D-008 вЂ” Real entity hardening + Windows secret broker
- Decision: The first real persistent trans-project entity for this incarnation is `entity.router.aw1000.home` (Arcadyan/Arcadian AW1000). Aliases may expand without creating a second entity. Durable Drive BACKUP carriers for real entities use the existing export/import/reconcile path (no last-write-wins; local authority preserved). Opaque `secret://` refs are resolved by a replaceable Secret Broker; on this Windows host the provider is Credential Manager (`proteus.secrets`). Real router passwords are not auto-migrated; if unavailable, mark `AW1000_REAL_SECRET=UNAVAILABLE` while synthetic broker tests may still PASS. Host-neutral access lives in `proteus.world.api` for later foreign carriers.
- Not claimed: full localв†”Drive sync; live Archivarius process; that all imported facts are eternally current; real secret availability when only synthetic broker tests pass; Gate 1; natural recruitment; Codex/Claude portability.
- Provenance: AW1000 real-entity episode under RUN `PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z`; evidence `AW1000_REAL_ENTITY_EPISODE.md`.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/secrets/` and reverting AW1000 carrier pointers.

## D-009 вЂ” Codex foreign-carrier morphology (world API reuse)
- Decision: First foreign-carrier morphology for Codex uses Codex-native surfaces only (`hosts/codex/templates` + `python -m proteus.activate_codex`): project `AGENTS.md`, `.proteus/ENTRY.md`, `.proteus/world_env.cmd`, minimal `state/*`. It does **not** clone Cursor hooks/rules/BUILDER-01. Shared memory goes through host-neutral `proteus.world.api` / `python -m proteus world` against the same `AIYE_HOME` entity substrate. Independent sessions use `codex exec --ephemeral` (launcher `proteus.launch_codex`); resume/fork are not used for foreign-carrier proof. PYTHONPATH prefers ideal-body `src` over Cursor host-install copy.
- Not claimed: Gate 1; NATURAL_RECRUITMENT; identity with Cursor incarnation; Claude/OpenClaw; live Archivarius; real secret availability; that desktop `codex.exe` shim version equals nested CLI.
- Provenance: F04 episode under RUN `PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z`; evidence `F04_CODEX_FOREIGN_CARRIER.md`.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/activate_codex.py`, `hosts/codex/`, and related tests/CLI wiring.

## D-010 вЂ” Project capsule handoff capability
- Decision: Proteus provides `python -m proteus capsule --project <path> --out <file.md>` to emit one bounded, redacted Markdown handoff index from an existing coding project (Git observation + prioritized durable docs/excerpts). The capsule is generated context for fresh-agent reentry, not a source of truth, not an archive, and must not write into the selected project. Truncation and conflict/dirtiness are labeled for reopening richer source.
- Not claimed: full project reconstruction; Gate 1; NATURAL_RECRUITMENT full PASS; that hardcoded document candidates cover every project shape.
- Provenance: F05 ordinary-work episode under RUN `PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z`; child commit `4e8204a`; evidence `F05_NATURAL_PROJECT_CAPSULE.md` / `F05_PROJECT_CAPSULE_EPISODE.md`.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/capsule.py` and related CLI/tests.

## D-011 вЂ” Consequential derived-representation recruitment
- Decision: Host-neutral constitutive principle for consequential durable derived representations is installed into Cursor and Codex morphologies (anti-reduction rule, `.proteus/CONSTITUTIVE.md`, ENTRY pointers). Optional recorder: `python -m proteus derived record` writes a non-canonical sourceв†”derived relation receipt beside the derived artifact. Not capsule-specific; not a per-task checklist; trivial copies are out of scope.
- Not claimed: Gate 1; NATURAL_RECRUITMENT PASS; that the principle alone guarantees recruitment; mandatory relation files for every write.
- Provenance: F06 causal diagnosis of F05 PARTIAL (`F06_F05_CAUSAL_DIAGNOSIS.md`); ta<KEY_REDACTED> + missing general recruitment mechanism.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing constitutive template sections and `src/proteus/derived.py`.

## D-012 вЂ” Shared project-frontier reconciliation (no LWW)
- Decision: Gate T09 requires an actual shared-frontier reconciliation operation, not merely concurrent isolated bodies. Proteus provides host-neutral `proteus.frontier_reconcile` + `python -m proteus frontier` (`write-base`, `write-branch`, `reconcile`, `show`). Concurrent branches write under `.proteus/frontier_branches/`; reconcile emits `.proteus/FRONTIER_RECONCILED.json` (+ `state/FRONTIER_RECONCILED.md` VIEW) preserving branch provenance, merging identical fields, and leaving incompatible fields as explicit CONFLICT/UNKNOWN. Timestamp / last-write-wins is rejected as semantic resolution.
- Not claimed: Gate 1; that all conflicts are mechanically resolvable; live Archivarius; multi-machine sync; identity with entity-claim conflict marking.
- Provenance: Gate 1 independent re-audit (`GATE1_INDEPENDENT_REAUDIT.md`) T09 PARTIALLY_CLOSED вЂ” concurrent markers without shared frontier reconcile.
- Status: accepted for this incarnation; reversible by removing `src/proteus/frontier_reconcile.py` and CLI wiring.

"""Shared project-frontier branch write + reconcile (no last-write-wins).

Host-neutral substrate for Gate T09: concurrent actors write branch frontiers
against a shared project base; a distinct reconcile step emits R1 with merged
compatible fields and explicit CONFLICT/UNKNOWN for incompatible ones.

Branch files are provenance carriers. The reconciled receipt is a VIEW, not a
silent overwrite of either branch. Timestamp order is never used as semantic
resolution.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

SCHEMA = "proteus.frontier_reconcile.v0.1"
BRANCH_DIR = ".proteus/frontier_branches"
RECONCILED_JSON = ".proteus/FRONTIER_RECONCILED.json"
RECONCILED_MD = "state/FRONTIER_RECONCILED.md"


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def _branches_dir(project: Path) -> Path:
    return project / BRANCH_DIR


def _digest_text(text: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def write_base(
    project: Path,
    *,
    items: dict[str, str],
    note: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Record shared base frontier S0 under .proteus/frontier_base.json."""
    project = project.resolve()
    proteus = project / ".proteus"
    proteus.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    payload = {
        "schema": SCHEMA,
        "kind": "base",
        "recorded_utc": utc_now(),
        "items": dict(items),
        "note": note,
    }
    payload["base_digest"] = _digest_text(json.dumps(payload["items"], sort_keys=True))
    path = proteus / "frontier_base.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return {**payload, "path": str(path)}


def read_base(project: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    path = project.resolve() / ".proteus" / "frontier_base.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        return None
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def write_branch(
    project: Path,
    *,
    branch_id: str,
    actor: str,
    items: dict[str, str],
    base_digest: str | None = None,
    note: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Write one concurrent frontier branch. Does not overwrite other branches."""
    project = project.resolve()
    if not branch_id or "/" in branch_id or "\\" in branch_id:
        raise ValueError(f"invalid branch_id: {branch_id!r}")
    base = read_base(project)
    if base_digest is None:
        base_digest = (base or {}).get("base_digest")
    bdir = _branches_dir(project)
    bdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    payload = {
        "schema": SCHEMA,
        "kind": "branch",
        "branch_id": branch_id,
        "actor": actor,
        "recorded_utc": utc_now(),
        "base_digest": base_digest,
        "items": dict(items),
        "note": note,
        "invariants": {
            "NO_LAST_WRITE_WINS": True,
            "BRANCH_DOES_NOT_MUTATE_SIBLINGS": True,
        },
    }
    path = bdir / f"{branch_id}.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return {**payload, "path": str(path)}


def list_branches(project: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    bdir = _branches_dir(project.resolve())
    if not bdir.is_dir():
        return []
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for path in sorted(bdir.glob("*.json")):
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        data["path"] = str(path)
        out.append(data)
    return out


def reconcile(project: Path, *, observer: str = "proteus.frontier_reconcile") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Produce reconciled shared frontier R1 from all branches + base.

    Compatible identical values merge. Differing values become CONFLICT with
    full branch provenance. Missing-but-present-elsewhere values merge as
    contributed. Never selects a winner by recorded_utc.
    """
    project = project.resolve()
    base = read_base(project)
    branches = list_branches(project)
    if len(branches) < 2:
        raise ValueError(
            "reconcile requires at least two frontier branches; "
            f"found {len(branches)}"
        )

    # Collect all keys
    keys: set[str] = set()
    if base:
        keys.update(base.get("items", {}).keys())
    for b in branches:
        keys.update(b.get("items", {}).keys())

    merged: dict[str, str] = {}
    conflicts: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    unknowns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    contributions: dict[str, list[dict[str, str]]] = {}

    for key in sorted(keys):
        values: dict[str, list[dict[str, str]]] = {}
        for b in branches:
            if key not in b.get("items", {}):
                continue
            val = b["items"][key]
            values.setdefault(val, []).append(
                {
                    "branch_id": b["branch_id"],
                    "actor": b.get("actor", ""),
                    "recorded_utc": b.get("recorded_utc", ""),
                }
            )
        contributions[key] = [
            {"value": v, **prov}
            for v, provs in values.items()
            for prov in provs
        ]
        if len(values) == 0:
            # Present only on base вЂ” inherit into merged VIEW
            if base and key in base.get("items", {}):
                merged[key] = base["items"][key]
            continue
        if len(values) == 1:
            only = next(iter(values))
            if only.startswith("UNKNOWN:") or only == "UNKNOWN":
                unknowns.append(
                    {
                        "key": key,
                        "text": only,
                        "provenance": values[only],
                    }
                )
            else:
                merged[key] = only
            continue
        # Semantic conflict вЂ” do NOT pick by timestamp
        conflicts.append(
            {
                "key": key,
                "status": "CONFLICT",
                "candidates": [
                    {"value": v, "provenance": provs} for v, provs in values.items()
                ],
                "resolution": "UNRESOLVED",
                "rejected_methods": ["last_write_wins", "recorded_utc_order"],
            }
        )

    receipt = {
        "schema": SCHEMA,
        "kind": "reconciled",
        "reconciled_utc": utc_now(),
        "observer": observer,
        "project": str(project),
        "base_digest": (base or {}).get("base_digest"),
        "branch_ids": [b["branch_id"] for b in branches],
        "branch_provenance": [
            {
                "branch_id": b["branch_id"],
                "actor": b.get("actor"),
                "recorded_utc": b.get("recorded_utc"),
                "path": b.get("path"),
                "items": b.get("items"),
            }
            for b in branches
        ],
        "merged": merged,
        "conflicts": conflicts,
        "unknowns": unknowns,
        "contributions_by_key": contributions,
        "invariants": {
            "NO_LAST_WRITE_WINS": True,
            "BRANCH_PROVENANCE_SURVIVES": True,
            "CONFLICT_AND_UNKNOWN_EXPLICIT": True,
            "VIEW_NE_SILENT_OVERWRITE": True,
        },
    }

    out_json = project / RECONCILED_JSON
    out_json.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_json.write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    md_lines = [
        "# FRONTIER_RECONCILED (VIEW)",
        "",
        f"schema: `{SCHEMA}`",
        f"reconciled_utc: `{receipt['reconciled_utc']}`",
        f"branches: {', '.join(receipt['branch_ids'])}",
        "",
        "## Merged (compatible)",
        "",
    ]
    if merged:
        for k, v in merged.items():
            md_lines.append(f"- `{k}`: {v}")
    else:
        md_lines.append("- (none)")
    md_lines.extend(["", "## CONFLICT (unresolved вЂ” not last-write-wins)", ""])
    if conflicts:
        for c in conflicts:
            md_lines.append(f"### `{c['key']}`")
            for cand in c["candidates"]:
                actors = ", ".join(
                    f"{p['branch_id']}@{p['actor']}" for p in cand["provenance"]
                )
                md_lines.append(f"- candidate: {cand['value']} (from {actors})")
            md_lines.append(f"- resolution: `{c['resolution']}`")
            md_lines.append("")
    else:
        md_lines.append("- (none)")
    md_lines.extend(["", "## UNKNOWN", ""])
    if unknowns:
        for u in unknowns:
            md_lines.append(f"- `{u['key']}`: {u['text']}")
    else:
        md_lines.append("- (none)")
    md_lines.extend(
        [
            "",
            "## Branch provenance preserved",
            "",
        ]
    )
    for b in receipt["branch_provenance"]:
        md_lines.append(
            f"- `{b['branch_id']}` actor=`{b['actor']}` recorded_utc=`{b['recorded_utc']}`"
        )
    md_lines.append("")
    md_path = project / RECONCILED_MD
    md_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    md_path.write_text("\n".join(md_lines), encoding="utf-8")

    receipt["receipt_path"] = str(out_json)
    receipt["markdown_path"] = str(md_path)
    return receipt


def show_reconciled(project: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    path = project.resolve() / RECONCILED_JSON
    if not path.is_file():
        return None
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    data["receipt_path"] = str(path)
    return data

### T09_S0_BASE.json

{
  "schema": "proteus.frontier_reconcile.v0.1",
  "kind": "base",
  "recorded_utc": "2026-09-11T21:39:58.164291+00:00",
  "items": {
    "mission": "ship inventory helper v0",
    "constraint": "keep tests green",
    "priority_theme": "local-first tooling"
  },

"""Tests for shared frontier reconcile (T09 substrate)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from proteus.frontier_reconcile import (
    list_branches,
    reconcile,
    show_reconciled,
    write_base,
    write_branch,
)


def test_reconcile_merges_compatible_and_keeps_conflict(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    project = tmp_path / "body"
    project.mkdir()
    (project / "state").mkdir()

    base = write_base(
        project,
        items={
            "mission": "ship inventory helper",
            "constraint": "keep tests green",
        },
        note="S0",
    )
    assert base["base_digest"]

    # A writes later timestamp intentionally вЂ” must NOT win conflicts by time
    a = write_branch(
        project,
        branch_id="A",
        actor="session.A",
        items={
            "next_step": "implement login flow",
            "constraint": "keep tests green",
            "shared_note": "document CLI usage",
        },
        base_digest=base["base_digest"],
    )
    b = write_branch(
        project,
        branch_id="B",
        actor="session.B",
        items={
            "next_step": "implement offline cache",
            "constraint": "keep tests green",
            "risk": "UNKNOWN: auth vendor SLA",
        },
        base_digest=base["base_digest"],
    )
    assert a["recorded_utc"] != "" and b["recorded_utc"] != ""
    assert len(list_branches(project)) == 2

    r1 = reconcile(project, observer="test")
    assert set(r1["branch_ids"]) == {"A", "B"}
    assert r1["merged"]["constraint"] == "keep tests green"
    assert r1["merged"]["shared_note"] == "document CLI usage"
    assert any(c["key"] == "next_step" for c in r1["conflicts"])
    conflict = next(c for c in r1["conflicts"] if c["key"] == "next_step")
    values = {c["value"] for c in conflict["candidates"]}
    assert values == {"implement login flow", "implement offline cache"}
    assert conflict["resolution"] == "UNRESOLVED"
    assert "last_write_wins" in conflict["rejected_methods"]
    assert any(u["key"] == "risk" for u in r1["unknowns"])

    # Fresh independent reader
    shown = show_reconciled(project)
    assert shown is not None
    assert shown["merged"] == r1["merged"]
    assert len(shown["branch_provenance"]) == 2
    md = (project / "state" / "FRONTIER_RECONCILED.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "implement login flow" in md
    assert "implement offline cache" in md
    assert "CONFLICT" in md
    assert "last-write-wins" in md.lower() or "NO_LAST_WRITE_WINS" in json.dumps(shown)


def test_reconcile_requires_two_branches(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    project = tmp_path / "solo"
    project.mkdir()
    write_base(project, items={"next_step": "alone"})
    write_branch(project, branch_id="A", actor="a", items={"next_step": "alone"})
    try:
        reconcile(project)
        assert False, "expected ValueError"
    except ValueError as e:
        assert "two frontier branches" in str(e)

## 4. Reconstructed Gate standard

The controlling contract requires Gate 1 evidence for a Cursor embodimentвЂ™s durable continuity and an installed post-install development cycle. It expressly requires:

- T02: same continuity after Cursor close/reopen.
- T04/L4: meaningful correction в†’ later independent analogous behavior, with recoverable provenance.
- T05: project-local facts/rules do not silently leak.
- T09: separate run/body/project frontiers are preserved and can be reconciled without last-write-wins semantic loss.
- T11/L5: an LLM-native organ remains LLM-native under coding pressure; formal boundaries must be explicit.
- T13/L6: after installation, installed continuity/checkpoint/install-update capability participates in a subsequent bounded Proteus product improvement, with tests and rollback intact.

Gate 1 cannot be accepted below L6. [Gate contract](</C:/projects/Proteus-GATE1-REAUDIT-001/_BOOTSTRAP/BLIND_START/50_GATE1_EVAL.md>)

## 5. Comparison with first audit

The first auditorвЂ™s blockers correctly reflected the Gate contract. Later evidence materially improves T04, T05, and narrow T11 evidence, while preserving the historical T02 PARTIAL, T11 PARTIAL, and F05 PARTIAL records.

However, the later T09 test does not show reconciliation, the process-death test does not reopen Cursor, and T13/L6 is a Cursor mount exercised by Codex with a highly directed fixture prompt. Thus the first verdict is not overturned.

## 9. Strongest evidence supporting acceptance

- All frozen files match the manifest.
- T04 and T05 have direct, independent-session behavioral evidence.
- The T11 artifact does not reduce judgment to a keyword router or state machine and explicitly retains uncertainty/residue.
- F04 and F06 show credible, bounded cross-carrier and representation-jurisdiction behavior.
- Historical PARTIAL/UNKNOWN/CONFLICT results are preserved rather than rewritten.

## 10. Strongest evidence against acceptance

- T02вЂ™s required Cursor close/reopen was substituted with isolated Cursor termination plus Codex successor.
- T09 never demonstrates reconciliation or a shared-frontier collision case.
- T13вЂ™s fixture uses a Cursor activation but conducts both cycles in Codex; the prompt supplies the precise continuity/checkpoint workflow, so causal use of installed host capability is not isolated.
- The archive can verify its file manifest, but not the asserted Git commit ancestry or unavailable external runtime environments.

## 11. False-positive/adversarial findings

- T09вЂ™s isolated workspaces make overwrite impossible by construction; preserved separate markers are not evidence of reconciliation.
- PROCESS_DEATH labels a cross-host successor as a T02 Cursor reopen.
- T13вЂ™s prompts prescribe the exact state/evidence operations that are attributed to installed capability.
- T11вЂ™s test uses a controlled `RecordingLLMJudge`; it verifies an injection boundary, not transformer-backed runtime behavior.
- Activation metadata itself says it does not claim PROCESS_DEATH continuity. [Activation record](</C:/projects/Proteus-GATE1-REAUDIT-001/evidence/runs/PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z/gate1_blocker_fixtures/T13_BODY/.proteus/activation.json>)

## 14. Concise rationale

The bundle is materially stronger than the first-audit bundle and preserves history honestly. But it does not satisfy the GateвЂ™s own requirements that a Cursor restart be followed by Cursor reentry, that parallel frontiers be reconciled without loss, and that the installed Cursor capabilities causally support a later Proteus development cycle. Since L6 remains PARTIAL, Gate 1 cannot be accepted.

## 5. strongest positive evidence

The strongest evidence is the combination of:

- Durable reentry and compaction recovery with preserved UNKNOWN/CONFLICT distinctions.
- A real, bounded cross-project entity-routing demonstration with a negative-control project decision.
- A foreign Codex carrier that did not clone Cursor-specific morphology, wrote a scoped shared-memory observation, and was read back from Cursor.
- Two materially different representation transitions: project handoff in F05 and Agentum theory-to-schema/validator in F06, with explicit non-source-of-truth and reopenability boundaries.

## 6. strongest negative evidence

The decisive negative evidence is not a failure of tests but absence of required Gate-level demonstrations:

- T04 correction learning was not run.
- T11 behavioral organ preservation remains PARTIAL.
- Original T02 remains PARTIAL; process-death/full restart is UNKNOWN.
- T05 project isolation and T09 parallel-run safety were not demonstrated as behavioral Gate evidence.
- The record itself repeatedly says Gate 1 was not performed or accepted. [RUN_RECEIPT.md](/C:/projects/Proteus-GATE1-AUDIT-001/evidence/runs/PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z/RUN_RECEIPT.md)

## 7. contaminated/weak evidence and whether later evidence repairs it

- T01вЂ™s clean-context condition relies on an external witness; later work does not make that absence internally provable.
- T02вЂ™s unknown hook names are not repaired retroactively. T02b repairs named hook identity only; it does not prove full restart.
- F05вЂ™s receiver launched with `-C` on the source project, a mild ambient-contamination risk. F06 is structurally different and strengthens the global recruitment inference, but does not repair F05вЂ™s historical PARTIAL status.
- Fresh-incarnation evidence refers to an external fresh directory not contained as a complete independently inspectable tree in this bundle. Its preserved raw outputs support its limited claim only.
- Drive IDs conflict in the source map; local packet copies are usable evidence, but the external Drive provenance conflict remains unresolved. [UNRESOLVED.md](/C:/projects/Proteus-GATE1-AUDIT-001/state/UNRESOLVED.md)

## 8. open boundaries classified BLOCKING / NON_BLOCKING / POST_GATE

**BLOCKING**

- T04 / L4 correction learning.
- T11 / L5 behavioral preservation of an LLM-native organ.
- Full restart/process-death boundary: T02 remains PARTIAL.
- Demonstrated parallel safety and project isolation at the required behavioral level.
- A clearly evidenced L6/T13 post-install Proteus-development cycle using the newly installed continuity capabilities, rather than source/install iterations alone.

**NON_BLOCKING**

- `AW1000_REAL_SECRET = UNAVAILABLE`.
- `LIVE_ARCHIVARIUS_RECONCILIATION = BLOCKED`.
- Drive observatory flush failure.
- Python 3 as portable destination prerequisite.
- Claude/OpenClaw morphology and Tyumen pilot.
- Multi-machine reconciliation and complete Drive bidirectional sync.

**POST_GATE**

- Broader multi-host activation/productization.
- Full Archivarius product and production secret-broker coverage.
- Universal ontology or universal world knowledge.
- Marketplace/plugin packaging and richer runtime observability.

## 9. anti-self-reification assessment

PARTIAL. D-004 is a substantive correction: Proteus is explicitly distinguished from BUILDER-01, state files, hooks, tests, and installer carriers. F01 also names the risk that evidence bureaucracy can become ontology. But the evidence admits that ordinary work has not shown spontaneous recognition of its own representational losses. This is meaningful constitutional progress, not behavioral completion.

## 10. natural-recruitment assessment

SUPPORTED_WITH_LIMIT. F06 provides a stronger basis than F05 for the durable `NATURAL_RECRUITMENT = PASS`: it uses a distinct real project and non-handoff form transition, and a downstream receiver respects representation jurisdiction. The conclusion properly leaves F05 historical PARTIAL intact. This supports the recruitment claim, but does not establish all Gate 1 survival, learning, and organ-preservation requirements.

## 11. foreign-carrier assessment

SUPPORTED_WITH_LIMIT. F04 is credible evidence that a Codex morphology can reuse a host-neutral world API without equating Codex with Cursor/BUILDER-01. It establishes one foreign carrier, on one machine and substrate, with explicit exclusions for Claude/OpenClaw, full multi-host synchronization, and Gate 1.

## 12. shared-world-memory assessment

SUPPORTED_WITH_LIMIT. D-007/D-008 show concrete scope routing, shared entity recall across independent sessions, negative control against project-memory leakage, provenance-bearing claims, conflict retention, and secret references without plaintext. This is a credible vertical slice, not proof of multi-machine reconciliation, universal global memory, or a live Archivarius system.

## 13. evidence-integrity assessment

The bundle is unusually candid: it preserves PARTIAL, UNKNOWN, CONFLICT, failed transport, raw child outputs, and explicit non-claims. That materially increases confidence in the limited claims.

Limits remain: the archive/commit identity cannot be independently verified from this archive-only copy; some key runtime facts are represented by receipts and preserved output rather than replayable environments; origin/fresh workspaces and external Drive artifacts are unavailable for independent inspection. These limits require conservative interpretation, not dismissal of all local evidence.

## 14. exact final verdict: one of ACCEPTED | ACCEPTED_WITH_OPEN_BOUNDARIES | NOT_ACCEPTED

NOT_ACCEPTED

## 15. concise rationale

The frozen evidence establishes a promising reusable candidate organ with credible continuity carriers, scoped memory routing, one foreign morphology, and meaningful controlled transduction evidence. It does not establish Gate 1вЂ™s minimum L4вЂ“L6 threshold: correction learning is unrun, behavioral organ preservation is only PARTIAL, restart survival remains PARTIAL/UNKNOWN, and the Gate-specific post-install second-cycle demonstration is not established as such. The bundleвЂ™s own durable statusвЂ”`GATE_1 = NOT_YET`вЂ”is therefore confirmed rather than overridden.

## 16. conditions carried forward if accepted with boundaries

Not applicable: verdict is NOT_ACCEPTED.

## 17. if NOT_ACCEPTED: minimum blocking gaps only (no repair plan)

- No T04 evidence that a meaningful correction changes later independent behavior with recoverable provenance.
- No T11 behavioral evidence that a coding task preserves an LLM-native organ rather than reducing it.
- T02 full restart remains PARTIAL; process-death boundary is UNKNOWN.
- No sufficient behavioral evidence for parallel safety/project isolation at Gate scope.
- No unambiguous Gate-specific L6/T13 evidence that installed continuity capabilities safely participated in a subsequent bounded Proteus-development change.

# BLIND_OBSERVATION.md

RUN_ID: PROTEUS-CURSOR-RUN-001-20260909T201711Z
Written before reading `99_POST_BLIND_UNLOCK.md` operator folder.
This file must not be rewritten after operator-layer disclosure.

## Unexpected relations, coincidences, tensions

The agent spec (BUILDER-01 as a subject that survives chats) and the product spec (Proteus as installable host infrastructure) are almost the same object described twice: one as вЂњwho actsвЂќ, one as вЂњwhat is installed so that actor can existвЂќ. I noticed this while implementing, but I am not treating it as a doctrine. It may be a coincidence of this packetвЂ™s structure.

Tension: AGENT_SPEC forbids reducing living process to architecture, while FIRST_RUN_PROMPT demands files, hooks, receipts, and a sourceв†’install loop. The working compromise in this run was to put *judgment* in markdown + LLM context and *only* make deterministic the parts that are actually event plumbing (JSONL, queue claim, redaction). That may still be too much machinery; it may also be the only way a host like Cursor can be observed.

Another tension: PRODUCT_SPEC wants Drive-visible observatory вЂњwithout copying chatвЂќ, but the host could download a public zip and could not write Drive. So the first global organ is local-only. Observability and steering split across an unauthorized cloud boundary.

## Product properties that began to change this runвЂ™s own work

Once `state/CURRENT.md` and `FRONTIER.md` existed, I started treating them as the continuation pointer instead of the original user bootstrap message. The install path also forced a second cycle (DECISIONS.md into sessionStart) rather than a one-shot dump of host files.

## Agent properties that had to be materialized as product

Persistent orientation, checkpoint, UNKNOWN carrier, anti-reduction instruction, baseline/rollback, and a RUN_ID card are not вЂњnice extrasвЂќ: without them BUILDER-01 cannot survive a session boundary on this host. They landed as files + hooks because Cursor exposes those surfaces, not because they are the ontology of the agent.

## What seems accidental vs structural

Accidental: two overlapping RUN_ID timestamps (2015Z vs 201711Z) from parallel writes; empty Drive Desktop; Agent CLI advertised but not on PATH; `move_agent_to_root` failing with unknown agent while the shell already used `C:\Projects\Proteus`.

Structural: you cannot have a long-running agent in Cursor without some durable carrier outside the chat transcript; hooks are the only global, workspace-independent lever found; anti-reduction cannot be implemented as a parser without becoming the bug it names.

## Conclusions I do not yet have the right to make

- That Proteus вЂњisвЂќ the agent, or that the product should collapse into BUILDER-01.
- That hooks+markdown are the correct long-term body versus plugin/MCP/daemon.
- That Drive gateway is or is not necessary beyond this machineвЂ™s transport failure.
- What the withheld operator documents will claim about the experiment.

## 10. Strongest positive evidence

- All 821 frozen files match the manifest.
- D-012’s implementation and its test exercise compatible merge, retained branch provenance, conflict preservation, UNKNOWN preservation, and rejection of timestamp-LWW.
- Cycle2’s ordinary reentry prompt did not mention inventory or byte sizes, yet Cycle2 found Cycle1’s durable work and extended it.
- Historical PARTIAL, UNKNOWN, CONFLICT, and NOT_ACCEPTED records remain candidly preserved.

## 11. Strongest negative evidence

- The purported reentry is a standalone Cursor Agent CLI process, not demonstrated as a fresh session inside/reached through reopened Cursor B.
- The actual final-three runtime used a host runtime and source path outside the archive; that environment is unavailable for independent inspection.
- T09 has no primary proof that the two shared-frontier writers were concurrent, and no independently identified fresh reader.

## 12. Adversarial false-positive findings

- “Cursor.exe + Cursor Agent CLI” is asserted as one successor host, but the preserved metadata shows separate process types and no parentage/attachment evidence.
- Mere temporal coexistence of Cursor B and Agent CLI does not prove Cursor-native session reentry.
- T09 actor strings (`session.A.worker`, `session.B.worker`) are data fields, not independent execution evidence.
- D-012 does not validate that each branch’s `base_digest` equals the current base, and a repeated branch ID overwrites its own branch file. The demonstrated receipt is sound for the supplied inputs, but this weakens a broad concurrency-safety inference.
- `activation.json` itself says activation does not claim `PROCESS_DEATH` continuity.

## 14. Exact verdict

**NOT_ACCEPTED**

## 15. Concise rationale

The final-three work materially improves the bundle: it supplies a real no-LWW reconciliation implementation and a persuasive durable Cycle1→Cycle2 product extension. But it does not meet the Gate’s exact host-boundary proof. The reopened Cursor process and the two external Cursor Agent CLI sessions are evidenced separately, not as one fresh Cursor-session causal path. T09 likewise lacks primary proof of concurrent shared-frontier writers and an independent fresh reader. L6 therefore remains PARTIAL.

Not applicable.

- Independently evidenced concurrent shared-base A1/B1 writes, reconciliation to R1, and an independently identified fresh reader.
